The calculation of the quantity in circulation shall be carried out in accordance with the provisions of Annex 8 number 2.[^48-3]

<sup>2</sup>

The FOEN may cancel the auction without accepting a bid if:

- a. it suspects agreements affecting competition or unlawful practices by dominant participants in the auction;
- b. the clearing price in the auction period differs significantly from the relevant price on the secondary market in the European Union; or
- c. safety risks or other reasons jeopardise the proper implementation of the auction.

<sup>3</sup> The FOEN must report any suspicions under paragraph 2 letter a to the competition authorities.

<sup>4</sup> If the auction is cancelled for any of the reasons in paragraph 2 or if demand for the quantity of emission allowances assigned to an auction is not fully met, the remaining emission allowances are transferred to a subsequent auction.

<sup>5</sup> The emission allowances which are not assigned to an auction are cancelled at the end of the obligation period.

<sup>6</sup> The FOEN may commission private organisations to conduct the auction.

[^48-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 24 Nov. 2021, in force since 1 Jan. 2022 ([AS 2021 859](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2021/859)).
[^48-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^48-3]: Inserted by No I of the O of 24 Nov. 2021, in force since 1 Jan. 2022 ([AS 2021 859](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2021/859)).

#### Art. 49

**Information to be submitted for participation**

<sup>1</sup>

Installation and aircraft operators participating in the Swiss and the European Union emissions trading systems and the other companies from the EEA admitted to bid in auctions in the European Union which are participating in the auction of emission allowances must submit the following information to the FOEN beforehand:

- a. first names, surnames, postal address, personal e-mail address, mobile telephone number, proof of identity and criminal record certificate of at least one, but no more than four, authorised auction agents;
- b. first names, surnames, postal address, personal e-mail address, mobile telephone number, proof of identity and criminal record certificate of at least one, but no more than four, bid validators;
- c. declaration that they and the authorised auction agents and bid validators accept the general conditions of the auction.

<sup>2</sup> Persons under paragraph 1 are not required to submit a Swiss criminal record certificate if they provide proof by means of notarial certificate that they have not been convicted of any of the criminal offences set out in Article 59*a* paragraph 1 letter b.

<sup>3</sup> Installation and aircraft operators obliged to participate in the ETS in the European Union must provide evidence of an operator account in the EU register and designate an address for service in Switzerland in addition to paragraph 1.

<sup>4</sup>

The companies from the EEA admitted to bid in auctions in the European Union must designate an address for service in Switzerland and submit the following information in addition to paragraph 1:

- a. evidence of direct admission to bid in auctions in the European Union;
- b. information on the categorisation in accordance with European Union regulations;
- c. an affirmation that they are participating in the auction solely on their own account.
- [tab] <sup>5</sup> The FOEN may request additional information if it requires it for the participation in the auction.
- [tab] <sup>6</sup> The proof of identity and criminal record certificates under paragraph 1 letters a and b and the information under paragraph 5 must be authenticated. Copies of documents issued outside of Switzerland must be apostilled. The date of the documents to be submitted and the authentication or apostille may not be more than three months before the date of application.

<sup>7</sup> The information is recorded in the Emissions Trading Registry.

#### Art. 49a

**Binding nature of the auction bids**

<sup>1</sup> Bids for the auction of emission allowances are made in euro and are binding after a bid validator gives consent.

<sup>2</sup> The invoice for the auctioned emission allowances must be settled in euro and via a bank account in Switzerland or in the EEA. The FOEN may exclude participants from future auctions if they fail to settle the invoice.

### Section 3 Data Collection and Monitoring

#### Art. 50

**Data collection**[^50-1]

<sup>1</sup>

The FOEN or a FOEN-authorised entity shall collect the data concerning installation operators required for:

- a. calculating the maximum quantity of emission allowances available each year to all installation operators in the ETS;
- b. the very first calculation of the quantity of emission allowances to be allocated free of charge.[^50-2]

<sup>1bis</sup> The operator shall collect the data required to adjust the quantity of emission allowances allocated free of charge under Article 46*b*.[^50-3]

<sup>2</sup> The installation operator is required to cooperate. If it violates its obligation to cooperate, then it shall be denied emission allowances free of charge.

<sup>3</sup> Aircraft operators are responsible for collecting the data relating to their activities under this Ordinance.

[^50-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^50-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^50-3]: Inserted by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).

#### Art. 51

**Monitoring plan**[^51-1]

<sup>1</sup> Installation operators participating shall submit a monitoring plan to the competent authority for approval in accordance with Annex 14 no later than three months after the deadline for notification under Article 40 paragraph 2 or after the submission of an application to participate under Article 42. They use the template provided or approved by the FOEN.[^51-2]

<sup>2</sup> Aircraft operators participating in the Swiss ETS shall submit a monitoring plan to the competent authority for approval in accordance with Annex 14 no later than three months after notification of the obligation to participate in accordance with Article 46*d* paragraph 2 for the first time. If the monitoring plan must be submitted to the FOEN, they use the template provided or approved by the FOEN.[^51-3]

<sup>3</sup> The monitoring plan must meet the requirements set out in Annex 16.

<sup>4</sup> The monitoring plan must be amended if it no longer meets the requirements of Annex 16. The amended monitoring plan must be submitted to the competent authority for approval in accordance with Annex 14.[^51-4]

<sup>5</sup> The CO<sub>2</sub> monitoring plan in accordance with the Ordinance of 2 June 2017[^51-5] on the acquisition of tonne-kilometre data and the drawing-up of monitoring plans for flight routes is deemed the monitoring plan.

[^51-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^51-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^51-3]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^51-4]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^51-5]: [[AS 2017 3477](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2017/378); [2019 1477](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/284)]

#### Art. 52

**Monitoring report**[^52-1]

<sup>1</sup> Installation or aircraft operators shall submit the monitoring report to the competent authority for each year by 31 March of the following year in accordance with Annex 14. If the monitoring report must be submitted to the FOEN, the template provided or approved by the FOEN must be used.[^52-2]

<sup>2</sup> The monitoring report must contain the relevant information in accordance with Annex 17. The FOEN may request additional information if required for monitoring.

<sup>3</sup> The FOEN may require at any time that the monitoring report of installation operators be verified by a FOEN-approved verifier.

<sup>4</sup> Aircraft operators must have their monitoring report verified by a verifier in accordance with Annex 18.

<sup>5</sup> The monitoring report of aircraft operators with CO<sub>2</sub> emissions that fall below the thresholds set out in Article 28*a* paragraph 6 of Directive 2003/87/EC[^52-3] is deemed to be verified if the aircraft operator uses an instrument in accordance with Article 55 paragraph 2 of the Implementing Regulation (EU) 2018/2066[^52-4].[^52-5]

<sup>6</sup> If a monitoring report contains errors, is incomplete or is not submitted by the deadline, the competent authority shall estimate the relevant emissions in accordance with Annex 14 at the expense of the installation or aircraft operator.[^52-6]

<sup>7</sup> If there are doubts about the correctness of the verified monitoring report, the competent authority may adjust the emissions at its own dutiful discretion in accordance with Annex 14.

<sup>8</sup> If the information in the monitoring report required for an adjustment under Article 46*b* is incorrect or incomplete, the FOEN shall fix an appropriate deadline for its rectification. If the monitoring report is not rectified by this deadline, no emission allowances shall be allocated free of charge to the sub-installations concerned for the year concerned.[^52-7]

[^52-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^52-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^52-3]: Directive 2003/87/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 13 October 2003 establishing a system for greenhouse gas emission allowance trading within the Community and amending Council Directive 96/61/EC, OJ L 275 of 25.10.2003, p. 32; last amended by Directive (EU) 2018/410, OJ L 76 of 19.3.2018, p. 3.
[^52-4]: Commission Implementing Regulation (EU) 2018/2066 of 19 December 2018 on the monitoring and reporting of greenhouse gas emissions pursuant to Directive 2003/87/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council and amending Commission Regulation (EU) No 601/2012, OJ L 334 of 31.12.2018, p. 1; last amended by Implementing Regulation (EU) 2020/2085, OJ L 423 of 15.12.2020, p. 37.
[^52-5]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^52-6]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^52-7]: Inserted by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).

#### Art. 53

**An ETS installation operator’s obligation to report changes**[^53-1]

<sup>1</sup>

ETS participants shall inform the competent authority without delay in accordance with Annex 14 about:

- a. changes that could affect the emission allowances to be allocated free of charge;
- b. changes in contact information.
- [tab] <sup>2</sup> Aircraft operators which no longer perform flights in accordance with Annex 13 must report this to the competent authority under Annex 14 no later than three months after cessation of the relevant flight activities.

<sup>3</sup>

Installation operators that carry on an activity in accordance with Annex 6 and that are exempt from the requirement to participate in the ETS shall notify the FOEN immediately:

- a. if the annual greenhouse gas emissions from the installations amount to 25 000 tonnes of CO<sub>2</sub>eq or more;
- b. of any changes to their contact details.[^53-2]

[^53-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^53-2]: Inserted by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).

#### Art. 54

**The cantons’ duties**

<sup>1</sup> The cantons shall verify whether installation operators have met their information obligations under Article 40 paragraph 2 and Article 53 paragraph 1 and 3 and whether the information provided is complete and traceable.[^54-1]

<sup>2</sup> The FOEN makes the required information available to the cantons.

<sup>3</sup> If a canton determines that the requirements of this Ordinance have not been met, it informs the FOEN without delay.

<sup>4</sup> The FOEN may consult the cantons in order to answer questions if this is necessary in order to implement the provisions on the ETS.[^54-2]

[^54-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^54-2]: Inserted by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).

### Section 4 Obligation to Surrender Emission Allowances

[^chap4-sec4-1]

[^chap4-sec4-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).

#### Art. 55

**Obligation**[^55-1]

<sup>1</sup> Each year installation operators shall surrender to the FOEN emission allowances. Decisive are the relevant greenhouse gas emissions of the installations that have been taken into account.[^55-2]

<sup>2</sup> Each year aircraft operators shall surrender to the competent authority under Annex 14 emission allowances. Decisive are the aircraft operator’s CO<sub>2</sub> emissions as recorded in accordance with Article 52.[^55-3]

<sup>2bis</sup> If an aircraft operator has to fulfil obligations under both the Swiss ETS and the EU ETS, the FOEN shall first count the emission allowances surrendered by the operators it manages towards fulfilling the obligation under the EU ETS.[^55-4]

<sup>3</sup> ETS participants shall meet these obligations each year by 30 April for the greenhouse gas emissions of the previous year.

[^55-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^55-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^55-3]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^55-4]: Inserted by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).

#### Art. 55a

**Case of hardship**[^55a-1]

<sup>1</sup>

On application, the FOEN may, in cases where European emission allowances are not recognised in the Swiss ETS in accordance with Article 4 paragraph 1 of the Agreement of 23 November 2017[^55a-2] between the Swiss Confederation and the European Union on the linking of their greenhouse gas emissions trading systems (ETS Agreement), count European emission allowances towards meeting the obligation of an ETS participant in accordance with Article 55 if the ETS participant proves that:

- a. it cannot meet its surrender obligation in accordance with Article 55 without these allowances being counted;
- b. it has participated in an auction of emission allowances in accordance with Article 48 and thus has made offers for the required quantity of emission allowances at market prices;
- c. procuring the lacking emission allowances issued by the Confederation in accordance with Article 45 paragraph 1 or Article 46*e* paragraph 1 outside auctions would significantly impair the ETS participant's competitiveness.

<sup>2</sup> To assess significant impairment to competitiveness, the FOEN shall also takes account in particular of the ETS participant's receipts from the sale of emission allowances issued by the Confederation.

<sup>3</sup> The application must be submitted to the FOEN no later than 31 March of the following year the year for which the case of hardship is claimed for the first time. The FOEN decides annually on the quantity of eligible European emission allowances.

<sup>4</sup> If no linking with the European Emissions Trading Registry exists or is likely to exist in the foreseeable future, the European emission allowances must be transferred annually to a Swiss Confederation account in the European Union Emissions Trading Registry.

[^55a-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)). Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^55a-2]: [SR 0.814.011.268](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/cc/2018/124)

### **55***b*–**55***d*

[^chap4-sec4-lvl55b55d-1]

[^chap4-sec4-lvl55b55d-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 13. Nov. 2019 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)). Repealed by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, with effect from 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).

#### Art. 56

**Non-compliance with obligations**

<sup>1</sup> If an ETS participant does not meet its obligations to surrender emission allowances by the deadline, then the FOEN shall impose a penalty in accordance with Article 21 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act.[^56-1]

<sup>2</sup> The payment deadline is 30 days from the issue of the ruling. If a payment is late, default interest is charged. The FDF shall fix the rate of interest.[^56-2]

<sup>3</sup> If an ETS participant does not surrender emission allowances by 31 January of the following year, then they shall be offset against the emission allowances allocated to the installation operator free of charge for that year.[^56-3]

[^56-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^56-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^56-3]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).

### Section 5 Emissions Trading Registry

[^chap4-sec5-1]

[^chap4-sec5-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014, in force since 1 Dec. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)).

#### Art. 57

**Principles**[^57-1]

<sup>1</sup> ETS participants must have an operator account in the Emissions Trading Registry; aircraft operators under the administration of a foreign authority in accordance with Annex 14 are exempted.

<sup>2</sup> Installation and aircraft operators participating in the European Union ETS and the other companies from the EEA admitted to bid in auctions in the European Union that wish to take part in the auction must have a personal account.

<sup>3</sup> Importers and manufacturers of fossil motor fuels in accordance with Chapter 7 that hold emission allowances, emission-reduction certifications or attestations in the Emissions Trading Registry, or that want to trade them, must have an operator or a personal account.[^57-2]

<sup>4</sup> All other companies and persons that hold emission allowances, emission-reduction certificates or attestations in the Emissions Trading Registry, or that want to trade them, must have a personal account.

<sup>5</sup> Companies, operators and persons that receive attestations for a project or a programme in accordance with Article 5, for emission reductions in accordance with Article 12, or for emission reductions arising from a target agreement with an emissions target in accordance with Article 12*a* may have them issued directly to a third party’s operator or personal account.

<sup>6</sup> A personal account holder may keep not more than a million emission allowances in his or her personal accounts.

[^57-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^57-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).

#### Art. 58

**Opening an account**[^58-1]

<sup>1</sup> Companies, operators and persons under Article 57 must apply to the FOEN to open an account.

<sup>2</sup>

The application must include:

- a. for installation or aircraft operators and other companies: an extract from the commercial register and a copy of the proof of identity of the person authorised to represent the operator or company;
- b. for natural persons: proof of identity;
- b<sup>bis</sup>.[^58-2] for competent authorities of a partner state: official confirmation from the government and a copy of the proof of identity of the person authorised to represent the operator or company;
- c. first names, surnames, postal and e-mail addresses and proof of identity of the applicant;
- d. first names, surnames, postal address, personal e-mail address, mobile phone number, proof of identity and criminal record certificate of at least one and no more than four authorised representatives for the account;
- e. first names, surnames, postal address, personal e-mail address, mobile phone number, proof of identity and criminal record certificate of at least one and no more than four transaction validators;
- f. declaration that the applicant accepts the General Terms and Conditions of the Emissions Trading Registry.

<sup>3</sup> The submission of a Swiss criminal record certificate is not required if evidence can be provided by means of notarial certificate that the persons concerned have not been convicted of the criminal offences set out in Article 59*a* paragraph 1 letter b.

<sup>4</sup> The FOEN may request additional information if required to open the account.

<sup>5</sup> Companies registered in a state in which no commercial register is maintained shall confirm by another form of supporting document their existence and the authorisation to sign of the person entitled to represent the company.

<sup>6</sup> Information on commercial register extracts, proof of identity, criminal record certificates and information in accordance with paragraphs 4 and 5 must be authenticated. Copies of documents issued outside of Switzerland must be apostilled. The date of the documents to be submitted and the authentication or apostille may not be more than three months before the application date.

<sup>7</sup> The FOEN opens the requested account after reviewing the information and documents and as soon as the applicant has paid the fee.

<sup>8</sup> Aircraft operators administered by the FOEN must submit an application to open an account in the Emissions Trading Registry within 30 working days after the approval of the aircraft operator’s monitoring plan or its transferral to Switzerland. The application must contain the unique aircraft code(s) of the aircraft operated by the applicant which fall under the Swiss ETS or the European Union ETS.

[^58-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^58-2]: Inserted by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).

#### Art. 59

**Address for service and registered office or domicile**[^59-1]

<sup>1</sup>

Any company or person with a personal account under Article 57 must designate an address for service in Switzerland for the following persons:

- a. for companies, the person entitled to represent the company, or for natural persons, the account holder;
- b. the authorised representatives for the account; and
- c. the transaction validators.

<sup>2</sup>

Any operator or person with an operator or personal account under Article 57 must designate an address for service in Switzerland or in the EEA for the following persons:

- a. the auction agents; and
- b. the bid validators.

<sup>2bis</sup> Any person or company resident or based in the United Kingdom may designate an address for service in the United Kingdom for persons under paragraph 2 instead of an address for service in Switzerland or in the EEA.[^59-2]

<sup>3</sup> A company that has an operator account or personal account in accordance with Article 57 must designate a registered office in Switzerland or in the EEA and hold a bank account in Switzerland or in the EEA.

<sup>4</sup> For an operator account or personal account of persons under Article 57, the account holder must designate a place of domicile in Switzerland or in the EEA and hold a bank account in Switzerland or in the EEA.

<sup>5</sup>

Paragraphs 3 and 4 do not apply:

- a. to accounts of aircraft operators outside of Switzerland and the EEA;
- b. to companies and persons based or registered in the United Kingdom, provided that they hold a bank account in Switzerland, the EEA or the United Kingdom;
- c.[^59-3] to the competent authority of a partner state.[^59-4]

[^59-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^59-2]: Inserted by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^59-3]: Inserted by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^59-4]: Amended by No I of the O of 25. Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).

#### Art. 59a

**Rejection of account opening**[^59a-1]

<sup>1</sup>

The FOEN shall reject the account opening or entry of authorised representatives for the account, authorised auction agents, transaction validators and bid validators if:

- a. the transmitted information or documents are incomplete, incorrect or not traceable;
- b. the company, the managing director or one of the persons mentioned in the introductory sentence has been convicted in the previous ten years of money laundering or criminal offences against property or of other criminal offences in connection with emissions trading or the legislation on financial market infrastructures, the financing of terrorism or other serious offences where the account was misused.
- [tab] <sup>2</sup> It shall suspend the account opening or entry if an investigation is pending against the company or a person mentioned in paragraph 1 letter b due to any of the criminal offences mentioned in paragraph 1 letter b.

<sup>3</sup> If the FOEN rejects the account opening of an installation or aircraft operator that is obliged to participate in the ETS, then the FOEN shall open a frozen account to which the emission allowances allocated under Article 46, 46*b* or 46*f* are credited. The account remains frozen until the reasons that led to the rejection of the account opening have been eliminated.[^59a-2]

[^59a-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)). Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^59a-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).

#### Art. 60

**Entry in the Emissions Trading Registry**[^60-1]

<sup>1</sup> All emission allowances, emission-reduction certificates, attestations and auction bids must be recorded in the Emissions Trading Registry.

<sup>2</sup> Changes in the holding of emission allowances, emission-reduction certificates and attestations are valid only if they are recorded in the Emissions Trading Registry.

<sup>3</sup>

Emission-reduction certificates for the following emission reductions may not be recorded in the Emissions Trading Registry:

- a. long-term certified emission reductions (lCER);
- b. temporary certified emission reductions (tCER);
- c. certified emission reductions from projects for CO<sub>2</sub> capture and geological CO<sub>2</sub> sequestration (CCS).

<sup>4</sup> The FOEN maintains a record of issuing attestations and emission allowances in the form of an electronic database.[^60-2]

[^60-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014, in force since 1 Dec. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)).
[^60-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).

#### Art. 61

**Transactions**[^61-1]

<sup>1</sup> Emission allowances, emission-reduction certificates and attestations are freely tradable.

<sup>2</sup> The authorised representatives for the account, authorised auction agents and the transaction validators and bid validators have the right to secure access to the Emissions Trading Registry.

<sup>3</sup>

When ordering a transaction involving emission allowances, emission-reduction certificates or attestations, authorised representatives for the account must give details of:

- a. the source and destination accounts; and
- b. type and quantity of emission allowances, emission-reduction certificates or attestations to be transferred.

<sup>4</sup> The emission allowances, emission-reduction certificates or attestations are transferred when the transaction validator consents to the transfer.

<sup>5</sup> The transaction is carried out according to a standardised procedure.

[^61-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014, in force since 1 Dec. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)).

#### Art. 62

**Registry management**[^62-1]

<sup>1</sup> The FOEN manages the Emissions Trading Registry electronically and records all transactions and auction bids.

<sup>2</sup> It ensures that it is possible to reproduce all the data relevant to transactions and auction bids at any time.

<sup>3</sup> In addition to the information submitted when an account is opened, it may also require further information at any time if necessary for the secure operation of the Registry.

<sup>4</sup> The FOEN shall review whether the information transmitted for the account opening is still complete, current and correct at least once every three years and requires the account holder to report any changes if applicable.[^62-2]

[^62-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014, in force since 1 Dec. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)).
[^62-2]: Inserted by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).

#### Art. 63

**Exclusion of liability**

The Confederation accepts no liability for any losses incurred due to:

- a.[^63-1] errors in transactions involving emission allowances, emission-reduction certificates, attestations and auction bids;
- b. restricted access to the Emissions Trading Registry;
- c. misuse of the Emissions Trading Registry by third parties.

[^63-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014, in force since 1 Dec. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)).

#### Art. 64

**Account freezing and closure**[^64-1]

<sup>1</sup> If the Emissions Trading Registry regulations are contravened or if an investigation is pending due to an offence under Article 59*a* paragraph 1 letter b, then the FOEN freezes the user access or accounts concerned. The freeze lasts until such time as the regulations are adhered to or the investigation is concluded.

<sup>2</sup>

The FOEN may close accounts:

- a. that do not contain any emission allowances, emission-reduction certificates or attestations and that have not been used for at least a year;
- b. whose holder or registered user has contravened the Emissions Trading Registry regulations for at least a year;
- c. if the annual account management fees have not been paid for over a year.[^64-2]

<sup>2bis</sup> The FOEN shall from 1 January 2026 close the operator accounts of operators with a reduction obligation in accordance with Article 31 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act. The operators concerned may open a personal account in accordance with Article 57 paragraph 4.[^64-3]

<sup>3</sup> If an account to be closed has a positive balance, the FOEN shall request the account holder to designate another account within 40 working days to which the units are to be transferred. If this request is not met, the FOEN shall cancel the applicable units.[^64-4]

[^64-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014, in force since 1 Dec. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)).
[^64-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^64-3]: Inserted by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^64-4]: Inserted by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).

#### Art. 65

**Publication of information and data protection**[^65-1]

The FOEN may, subject to preservation of manufacturing and trade secrecy, electronically publish the following data held in the Emissions Trading Registry:

- a. account number;
- b.

  for the following persons, contact details and data in accordance with proof of identity:

  - 1\. persons in accordance with Article 57 paragraphs 1–4,
  - 2\. bid validators,
  - 3\. authorised auction agents,
  - 4\. authorised representatives for the account,
  - 5\. transaction validators;

- c. emission allowances, emission-reduction certificates and attestations per account;
- c<sup>bis</sup>. transactions;
- d.[^65-2] for ETS participants: installation, aircraft and emissions data, the quantity of emission allowances allocated free of charge, the quantity of emission allowances and emission-reduction certificates delivered to meet their obligation;
- d<sup>bis</sup>.[^65-3] for aircraft operators that were administered by a foreign authority before the ETS Agreement[^65-4] came into force: aircraft and emissions data, the quantity of emission allowances allocated free of charge, the quantity of emission allowances and emission reduction certificates surrendered to meet their obligation, in each case since 2012 at the earliest;
- d<sup>ter</sup>.[^65-5] for auctions: auction bids, auction date and quantity, minimum and maximum bid quantity, purchase price and purchased quantity, participants in the auction;
- e.[^65-6] for projects and programmes for domestic and foreign emission-reductions or increases in the carbon sink effect: the quantity of attestations issued per monitoring period and account number of the operator or personal accounts to which the attestations for the project or programme have been issued;
- f.[^65-7] for persons with compensation obligations: the amount of the compensation obligation and the quantity of attestations, emission-reduction certificates and emission allowances delivered to meet the obligation;
- g.[^65-8] for operators with reduction obligations: the quantity of emission-reduction certificates and emission allowances delivered to meet the obligation.

[^65-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^65-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^65-3]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^65-4]: [SR 0.814.011.268](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/cc/2018/124)
[^65-5]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^65-6]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^65-7]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^65-8]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).

## Chapter 5 Commitment to reduce Greenhouse Gas Emissions

#### Art. 66

**Requirements**[^66-1]

<sup>1</sup>

In accordance with Article 31 paragraph 1 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act, an installation operator may commit to reduce its greenhouse gas emissions (operators with reduction obligations) if it:[^66-2]

- a. is engaged in an activity listed in Annex 7;
- b. produces at least 60 per cent of its greenhouse gas emissions due to an activity listed in Annex 7; and
- c. has emitted a total of more than 100 tonnes CO<sub>2</sub>eq of greenhouse gases in one of the preceding two years.

<sup>2</sup> The extent to which greenhouse gas emissions are reduced is determined by means of an emissions target or a measures target.

<sup>3</sup>

Two or more installation operators may make a joint commitment to reduce greenhouse gas emissions if:

- a. each of them is engaged in an activity listed in Annex 7;
- b. the source of at least 60 per cent of each of their greenhouse gas emissions is an activity listed in Annex 7; and
- c. together they have emitted more than 100 tonnes CO<sub>2</sub>eq of greenhouse gases in one of the preceding two years.[^66-3]

<sup>4</sup> The installation operators referred to in paragraph 3 are deemed a single operator. They must designate a representative.[^66-4]

[^66-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014, in force since 1 Dec. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)).
[^66-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^66-3]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^66-4]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).

#### Art. 67

**Emissions target**

<sup>1</sup> The emissions target is the maximum total amount of greenhouse gases that an installation operator may emit by the end of 2020.[^67-1]

<sup>2</sup> The FOEN calculates the emissions target on the basis of a linear reduction course.

<sup>3</sup>

The linear reduction course is based on Article 31 paragraph 3 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act and:

- a.[^67-2] on the greenhouse gas emissions of the installations in the preceding two years;
- b.[^67-3] on the state of the art of the technology used in the installations;
- c. on the already realised greenhouse-gas-effective measures and their effect;
- d. on the remaining reduction potential;
- e. on the economic efficiency of the possible greenhouse-gas-effective measures;
- f.[^67-4] ...
- g. on the portion of district heating or cooling produced;
- h. on the extent to which CO<sub>2</sub> levies can be saved.

<sup>4</sup> An installation operator that was under a reduction obligation in the years 2008–12 and would like to seamlessly continue from 2013 may apply for a simplified determination of the reduction course.[^67-5]

<sup>5</sup> A simplified determination of the reduction course is based on the greenhouse gas emissions of the installations in 2010 and 2011 and Article 3 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act. Insofar as installation operators have achieved additional reductions that exceed their commitments in the years 2008–12, this will be taken into account in determining the reduction course, except for additional reductions achieved as the result of using waste fuels.[^67-6]

[^67-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^67-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^67-3]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^67-4]: Repealed by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, with effect from 1 Jan. 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^67-5]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^67-6]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).

#### Art. 68

**Measures target**

<sup>1</sup> An operator whose installations normally emit no more than 1500 tonnes CO<sub>2</sub>eq per year may request that the extent of its reduction be determined by means of a measures target.[^68-1]

<sup>2</sup> The measures target includes the total amount of greenhouse gas emissions that the installation operator must reduce by the end of 2020 by means of measures.[^68-2]

<sup>3</sup>

The measures target is determined based on Article 31 paragraph 3 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act and:

- a.[^68-3] on the state of the art of the technology used in the installations;
- b. the remaining reduction potential;
- c. on the economic efficiency of the possible greenhouse-gas-effective measures;
- d.[^68-4] ...
- e. on the portion of district heating or cooling produced;
- f. on the extent to which CO<sub>2</sub> levies can be saved.

[^68-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^68-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^68-3]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^68-4]: Repealed by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, with effect from 1 Jan. 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).

#### Art. 69

**Application for the determination of a reduction obligation**

<sup>1</sup> An application for the determination of a reduction obligation must be submitted to the FOEN by 1 September of the previous year. On request, the FOEN may appropriately extend the application deadline. It issues guidelines on the form of the application.[^69-1]

<sup>2</sup>

The application must contain information about:

- a. the activities listed in Annex 7;
- b. the greenhouse gas emissions and production volumes of the preceding two years;
- c. the emissions target or measures target that the installation operator strives for.

<sup>2bis</sup> The proposal for the measures target must be prepared in consultation with one of the private organisations commissioned by the FOEN in accordance with Article 130 paragraph 6.[^69-2]

<sup>3</sup>

The FOEN may request additional information if required for the determination of reduction obligations, particularly about:

- a.[^69-3] the state of the art of the technology used in the installations;
- b.[^69-4] already implemented greenhouse-gas-effective measures, their effect and financing;
- c. the possible technical and economic greenhouse-gas-effective measures, with an evaluation of their effect and costs.

<sup>4</sup> It may request the installation operator to submit a monitoring plan in accordance with Article 51.[^69-5]

[^69-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014, in force since 1 Dec. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)).
[^69-2]: Inserted by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)). Amended by No I of the O of 22 June 2016, in force since 1 Aug. 2016 ([AS 2016 2473](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2016/394)).
[^69-3]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^69-4]: Amended by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014, in force since 1 Dec. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)).
[^69-5]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).

#### Art. 70

**Ruling**

The FOEN rules on reduction obligations.

#### Art. 71

**Product improvements outside an installation operator’s own production plants**

<sup>1</sup>

Emission reductions that an installation operator has achieved due to product improvements outside its own production plants may on request be taken into account towards meeting reduction obligations if they are:

- a. analogous with the requirements of Article 5; and
- b. directly related to the installation operator’s activity.

<sup>2</sup> The procedure is described in Articles 6–11.

#### Art. 72

**Monitoring report**[^72-1]

<sup>1</sup> An installation operator must submit annually by 31 May of the following year a monitoring report to a private organisation commissioned by the FOEN in accordance with Article 130 paragraph 6. If cooperation with the private organisations ends, the operator shall submit the monitoring report to the SFOE. The private organisations commissioned or the SFOE shall forward the monitoring report to the FOEN.[^72-2]

<sup>2</sup>

The monitoring report must contain:

- a. information about the progression of greenhouse gas emissions;
- b. information about the progression of production volumes;
- c. an accounting of thermal fuels;
- d. a description of implemented greenhouse-gas-effective measures;
- e. information about possible deviations from the reduction course or measures target with a justification and planned corrective measures.

<sup>3</sup> The data must be contrasted in a summary table with comparative data from previous years. The FOEN defines the form of the monitoring report in a directive.

<sup>4</sup> The FOEN may request additional information if required for monitoring.

[^72-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014, in force since 1 Dec. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)).
[^72-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).

#### Art. 73

**Amendment of the emissions target**

<sup>1</sup>

The FOEN shall amend the emissions target if the installations’ greenhouse gas emissions exceed or fall short of the reduction course due to a significant and permanent change in production amount or product mix or due to the procurement of heating or cooling from a third party:[^73-1]

- a. in three consecutive years by at least 10 per cent per year; or
- b. in one year by at least 30 per cent.

<sup>2</sup> It amends the emissions target retroactively from the beginning of the year in which the installation operator’s greenhouse gas emissions first exceeded or fell short of the reduction course.

<sup>3</sup> It takes account of the criteria in Article 67 paragraph 3.

[^73-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).

#### Art. 74

**Amendment of the measures target**

<sup>1</sup> The FOEN shall amend the measures target if the installations’ greenhouse gas emissions change significantly due to a change in production amount or product mix or due to the procurement of heating or cooling from a third party.[^74-1]

<sup>2</sup> It takes account of the criteria in Article 68 paragraph 3.

[^74-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).

#### Art. 74a

**Deducting attestations from the emissions target**[^74a-1]

Emission reductions for which attestations in accordance with Articles 5 or 12 paragraph 2 have been issued shall be considered to be the greenhouse gas emissions of the installation operators concerned with a view to meeting the emissions target.

[^74a-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 1 Nov. 2017 ([AS 2017 6753](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2017/757)). Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).

#### Art. 74b

**Adjustment of the reduction obligation of CHP plant operators[^74b-2]**[^74b-1]

<sup>1</sup> On request, the FOEN shall adjust the reduction obligation of CHP plant operators that apply for a refund of the CO<sub>2</sub> levy in accordance with Article 96*a*.[^74b-3]

<sup>2</sup> Applications must be submitted to the FOEN by 31 May of the following year.

<sup>3</sup>

Applications must contain information on:

- a. CO<sub>2</sub> emissions in 2012 resulting from the measured production of electricity fed into the grid;
- b. the annual development of CO<sub>2</sub> emissions resulting from the measured production of electricity fed into the grid.

<sup>4</sup> The FOEN shall specify the form of the application.

[^74b-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 1 Nov. 2017, in force since 1 Jan. 2018 ([AS 2017 6753](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2017/757)).
[^74b-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^74b-3]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).

#### Art. 75

**Counting emission reduction certificates**[^75-1]

<sup>1</sup>

An installation operator that has not reached its emissions target or measures target and has not been issued attestations in accordance with Article 12 may have emission reduction certificates taken into account towards meeting its reduction obligations to the following extent:

- a. for installation operators that were already subject to a reduction obligation in the years 2008–2012: 8 per cent of five times the average allowed emissions annually in this period, minus the emission reduction certificates that were taken into account during 2008-2012 but that were not required for meeting the operator’s 2008–2012 reduction obligations;
- b. for the remaining installation operators and greenhouse gas emissions: 4.5 per cent of the greenhouse gas emissions for 2013–2020;
- c.[^75-2] for installation operators that extend their reduction obligation under Article 31 paragraph 1<sup>bis</sup> of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act until the end of 2021: 4.5 per cent of the greenhouse gas emissions for 2013–2021.

<sup>2</sup>

The extent to which emission-reduction certificates are taken into account in accordance with paragraph 1 is as follows:

- a. for an installation operator that was only intermittently subject to a reduction obligation in the years 2013–2020: correspondingly reduced for this time period;
- b.[^75-3] for an installation operator that, in comparison with 2012, produces additional electricity by 2021 that is used outside the installations: increased by 50 per cent of the required increase in additional reduction performance;
- c. for an installation operator under paragraph 1 letter a, the emissions or measures target of which has been amended: increased or reduced in accordance with the amendment; the quantity of eligible emission-reduction certificates are thereby reduced to a maximum of 8 per cent of five times the average allowed emissions annually in this period, minus the emission-reduction certificates that were taken into account in the years 2008–2012.

[^75-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^75-2]: Inserted by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^75-3]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).

#### Art. 76

**Failure to meet reduction obligations and the investment obligation[^76-1]**

<sup>1</sup> If an operator fails to meet its reduction obligation, then the FOEN shall impose a penalty in accordance with Article 32 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act.[^76-2]

<sup>1bis</sup> If a CHP plant operator fails to meet the investment obligation in accordance with Article 96*a* paragraph 2 or Article 98*a* paragraph 2, the FOEN shall order the repayment of 40 per cent of the refund paid for fuels used for electricity production in accordance with Article 32*a* of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act.[^76-3]

<sup>2</sup> The payment deadline is 30 days from the entry of the ruling. If a payment is late, default interest is charged. The FDF shall fix the rate of interest.[^76-4]

<sup>3</sup> The amounts repaid in accordance with paragraph 1<sup>bis</sup> shall be deemed to be revenue from the CO<sub>2</sub> levy.[^76-5]

[^76-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 1 Nov. 2017, in force since 1 Jan. 2018 ([AS 2017 6753](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2017/757)).
[^76-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^76-3]: Inserted by No I of the O of 1 Nov. 2017 ([AS 2017 6753](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2017/757)). Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^76-4]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^76-5]: Inserted by No I of the O of 1 Nov. 2017, in force since 1 Jan. 2018 ([AS 2017 6753](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2017/757)).

#### Art. 77

**Security for the penalty**

If an installation operator is at risk of not meeting its target, then the FOEN may require security for the expected penalty until the risk no longer exists.

#### Art. 78

**Obligation to give notice of changes[^78-1]**

An installation operator shall notify the FOEN without delay about:[^78-2]

- a. changes that could affect its reduction obligations;
- b. changes in contact information.

[^78-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^78-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).

#### Art. 79

**Publication of information**[^79-1]

The FOEN may subject to the preservation of manufacturing and trade secrecy, publish:

- a. the names of installation operators with reduction obligations or the CHP plant operators;
- b. the emissions targets or measures targets;
- c. the greenhouse gas emissions of each installation;
- d. the extent to which emission reductions were taken into account in meeting each installation operator’s reduction obligation in accordance with Article 71;
- e.[^79-2] the quantity of emission-reduction certificates or emission allowances that each installation operator surrenders;
- f. the quantity of credits that have been taken into account towards meeting each installation operator’s reduction obligations in accordance with Article 138 paragraph 1 letter b;
- g. the quantity of attestations that have been issued to each installation operator in accordance with Article 12;
- h the extent of the investment made in accordance with Article 96*a* paragraph 2 or Article 98*a* paragraph 2;
- i.[^79-3] the private organisation commission in accordance with Article 69 paragraph 2<sup>bis</sup>.

[^79-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^79-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^79-3]: Inserted by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).

## Chapter 6 ...

#### Art. 80–85

[^8085-1]

[^8085-1]: Repealed by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, with effect from 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).

## Chapter 7 Compensation of CO<sub>2</sub> Emissions from Motor Fuels

#### Art. 86

**Compensation obligation**

<sup>1</sup>

Persons or installation operators are subject to compensation obligations if:

- a. they release motor fuels for consumption in accordance with Annex 10; or
- b. they convert fossil gases for combustion purposes to gases for use as motor fuels in accordance with Annex 10.

<sup>2</sup> CO<sub>2</sub> emissions from motor fuels that, in accordance with Article 17 of the Mineral Oil Tax Act of 21 June 1996[^86-1], are entirely exempt from mineral oil tax, need not be compensated.

[^86-1]: [SR 641.61](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/cc/1996/3371_3371_3371)

#### Art. 87

**Exemptions from the compensation obligation for small quantities**

<sup>1</sup> The obligations in Article 86 paragraph 1 do not apply to persons who, in the preceding three years, have released such small quantities of motor fuels for consumption that their use as energy has resulted in emissions of less than 1000 tonnes CO<sub>2</sub> per year.

<sup>2</sup> The exemption from the compensation obligation extends until the beginning of the year in which the CO<sub>2</sub> emissions resulting from the use as energy of motor fuels for consumption exceed 1000 tonnes.

#### Art. 88

**Compensation pools**

<sup>1</sup> Persons with compensation obligations may apply to the FOEN each year by 30 November of the previous year to be treated as a compensation pool.

<sup>2</sup> A compensation pool has the rights and obligations of an individual person with compensation obligations.

<sup>3</sup> It has a designated representative.

#### Art. 89

**Compensation rate**[^89-1]

<sup>1</sup> CO<sub>2</sub> emissions that result from the use as energy of motor fuels released for consumption in the relevant year must be compensated.

<sup>2</sup> The domestic compensation rate from 2022 amounts to at least 15 per cent.

<sup>3</sup>

The compensation rate in total amounts to:

- a. for 2022: 17 per cent;
- b. for 2023: 20 per cent;
- c. from 2024: 23 per cent.

<sup>4</sup> The CO<sub>2</sub> emissions of each motor fuel are calculated using the emission factors listed in Annex 10.

[^89-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 Jan. 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).

#### Art. 90

**Permissible compensation measures**[^90-1]

<sup>1</sup> Certificates for emission reductions or increasing the carbon sink effect may be surrendered in order to meet the compensation obligation.

<sup>2</sup> If the surrendered attestations no longer meet the permanence requirements of Article 5 paragraph 2, they may be counted towards meeting the compensation obligation.

<sup>3</sup> If attestations under paragraph 2 have already been counted towards meeting the compensation obligation, they shall be marked accordingly and refunded to the person required to compensate. The person required to compensate must submit additional attestations that meet the requirements of Article 5 in the following year in the same quantity. Attestations that could be submitted at the time of the original surrender may be submitted later.

[^90-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 Jan. 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).

#### Art. 91

**Meeting the compensation obligation**

<sup>1</sup> The person required to compensate shall meet their compensation obligation by 31 December of the year following the year in question.[^91-1]

<sup>2</sup> ...[^91-2]

<sup>3</sup> ... [^91-3]

<sup>4</sup> To meet a compensation obligation, the installation operator or person submits a detailed and transparent report on the costs of each tonne CO<sub>2</sub> compensated for.[^91-4]

<sup>5</sup>

The following data and documents are managed in a FOEN-administered database per person with a compensation obligation:

- a. the extent of the compensation obligation;
- b. the monitoring reports and verification reports of the self-implemented projects or programmes;
- c. emission reductions from self-implemented projects or programmes that are accounted-for;
- d. the quantity of emission reductions from self-implemented projects or programmes that have not yet been used for compensation;
- e. the quantity of emission reductions that have not yet been for compensation;
- f. information about the costs of each tonne CO<sub>2</sub> compensated;
- g. development and operating costs of self-implemented projects or programmes.[^91-5]

[^91-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13. Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^91-2]: Repealed by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, with effect from 1 Jan. 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^91-3]: Repealed by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, with effect from 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^91-4]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^91-5]: Inserted by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014, in force since 1 Dec. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)).

#### Art. 92

**Failure to meet a compensation obligation**

<sup>1</sup> If an installation operator or person obliged to compensate does not do so by the deadline, then the FOEN grants an appropriate grace period.

<sup>2</sup> If an installation operator or person obliged to compensate does not do so within the grace period, then the FOEN rules on a penalty in accordance with Article 28 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act.

<sup>3</sup> The payment deadline is 30 days from the issue of the ruling. If a payment is late, default interest is charged. The FDF shall fix the rate of interest.[^92-1]

<sup>4</sup> In the event of failure to compensate in accordance with Article 28 paragraph 2 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act, the emission reduction certificates, emission allowances or international attestations must be surrendered to the Confederation by 1 June of the following year.[^92-2]

[^92-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 Jan. 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^92-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 Jan. 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).

## Chapter 8 CO<sub>2</sub> Levy

### Section 1 General Provisions

#### Art. 93

**Subject to the levy**

The production, extraction and import of the following are subject to the CO<sub>2</sub> levy:

- a. coal;
- b. other thermal fuels listed in Article 2 paragraph 1 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act insofar as they are subject to a mineral oil tax under the Mineral Oil Tax Act of 21 June 1996[^93-1].

[^93-1]: [SR 641.61](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/cc/1996/3371_3371_3371)

#### Art. 94

**Rate of the levy**

<sup>1</sup>

The levy shall be increased as follows:

- a. from 1 January 2014: at 60 francs per tonne of CO<sub>2</sub>, if the CO<sub>2</sub> emissions from thermal fuels in 2012 exceed 79 per cent of 1990 emissions;
- b.

  from 1 January 2016:

  - 1\. at 72 francs per tonne of CO<sub>2</sub> if the CO<sub>2</sub> emissions from thermal fuels in 2014 exceed 76 per cent of 1990 emissions,
  - 2\. at 84 francs per tonne of CO<sub>2</sub> if the CO<sub>2</sub> emissions from thermal fuels in 2014 exceed 78 per cent of 1990 emissions;

- c.

  from 1 January 2018:

  - 1\. at 96 francs per tonne of CO<sub>2</sub> if the CO<sub>2</sub> emissions from thermal fuels in 2016 exceed 73 per cent of 1990 emissions,
  - 2\. at 120 francs per tonne of CO<sub>2</sub> if the CO<sub>2</sub> emissions from thermal fuels in 2016 exceed 76 per cent of 1990 emissions;

- d.[^94-1] from 1 January 2022: at 120 francs per tonne of CO<sub>2</sub> if the CO<sub>2</sub> emissions from thermal fuels in 2020 exceed 67 per cent of the 1990 emissions.

<sup>2</sup> The CO<sub>2</sub> levy is imposed in accordance with the tariffs listed in Annex 11.

[^94-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).

#### Art. 95

**Declaration of payment of the levy**[^95-1]

Any installation operator or person that trades in thermal fuels in accordance with Article 93 must declare the quantity of thermal fuels on which the CO<sub>2</sub> levy is paid and the applicable rate of the levy on invoices submitted to buyers.

[^95-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).

### Section 2 Refund of the CO<sub>2</sub> Levy

#### Art. 96

**Claim for refund**

<sup>1</sup>

The following installation operators and persons may apply for a refund of the CO<sub>2</sub> levy:[^96-1]

- a. those exempt from the CO<sub>2</sub> levy;
- b. those that operate CHP plants, which neither participate in the ETS nor are subject to a reduction obligation (Art. 32*a* para. 1 CO<sub>2</sub> Act); or
- c. those that have paid a levy on thermal fuels that were not used to produce energy (Art. 32*c* CO<sub>2</sub> Act).[^96-2]

<sup>2</sup>

The following are exempt from the CO<sub>2</sub> levy:

- a. installation operators participating in the ETS (Art. 17 CO<sub>2</sub> Act);
- b. *repealed*
- c. installation operators with reduction obligations (Art. 31 CO<sub>2</sub> Act).[^96-3]

[^96-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^96-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 1 Nov. 2017, in force since 1 Jan. 2018 ([AS 2017 6753](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2017/757)).
[^96-3]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).

#### Art. 96a

**Refund for CHP plant operators with a reduction obligation[^96a-2]**[^96a-1]

<sup>1</sup>

On request, a CHP plant operator with a reduction obligation shall be refunded 60 per cent of the CO<sub>2</sub> levy on fuel used for electricity production in accordance with Article 32*a* of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act if:[^96a-3]

- a. one or more CHP plants each have a rated thermal input of at least 0.5 MW and no more than 20 MW;
- b. one or more CHP plants produced an additional 1.22GWh per year of fossil fuel electricity per year compared to 2012; and
- c.[^96a-4] the additional electricity produced was used outside the plants.

<sup>2</sup>

It is entitled to the refund of the remaining 40 per cent of the CO<sub>2</sub> levy on the fuels used for electricity production in accordance with Article 32*a* of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act if:[^96a-5]

- a. it allocates this amount to the measures set out in Article 31*a* paragraph 2 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act;
- b. the measure effectively serves to increase energy efficiency;
- c.[^96a-6] it does not implement the measures in another plant whose operator is subject to a reduction obligation or is participating in the ETS;
- d. it does not claim the effect of the measures elsewhere;
- e.[^96a-7] it implements the measures by 2021;
- f. it regularly reports to the FOEN in accordance with Article 72; and
- g. it notifies the FOEN of any derogations from the investment obligation in accordance with letter a with reasons and details of the planned corrective measures.
- [tab] <sup>3</sup> On application, the FOEN may extend the period referred to in paragraph 2 letter e by two years.

[^96a-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 1 Nov. 2017, in force since 1 Jan. 2018 ([AS 2017 6753](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2017/757)).
[^96a-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^96a-3]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^96a-4]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^96a-5]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^96a-6]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^96a-7]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).

#### Art. 96b

**Refund for operators of fossil-thermal power plants**[^96b-1]

<sup>1</sup> On request, an operator of fossil-thermal power plants shall be refunded the difference between the CO<sub>2</sub> levy on thermal fuels paid and the minimum price in accordance with Article 17 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act.

<sup>2</sup>

Fossil-thermal power plants are installations that produce either solely electricity or also heat simultaneously from fossil energy sources:

- a. that are participating in the ETS for the first time after the amendment of 13 November 2019 comes into force;
- b. that have a total output of at least one MW and an overall efficiency of less than 80 per cent;
- c. that sell electricity to third parties;
- d. that are operated at a location for at least two years or for more than 50 hours per year;
- e. that are not used exclusively for the research, development and testing of new products and processes; and
- f. that are not used primarily for the disposal of municipal or special waste in accordance with Article 3 letters a and c respectively ADWO[^96b-2].

<sup>3</sup> In the evaluation of external costs in accordance with Article 17 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act the FOEN takes into account the current state of scientific knowledge in particular.

<sup>4</sup> Operators of fossil-thermal power plants shall submit the refund application to the FOEN by 30 June for the attention of the implementation authorities. The application must contain the prices for the purchase of emission allowances in the preceding twelve months. The relevant documents must be attached.

- [tab] <sup>5</sup> If the operator does not submit any verifiable information on the amounts paid, a value of zero francs shall be assumed.[^96b-3]

[^96b-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^96b-2]: [SR 814.600](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/cc/2015/891)
[^96b-3]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).

#### Art. 97

**Application for refund[^97-1]**

<sup>1</sup> An application for refund must be submitted to the Federal Office for Customs and Border Security (FOCBS)[^97-2] in the prescribed form.

<sup>2</sup>

It must include:

- a. a complete compilation of the CO<sub>2</sub> levy paid;
- b.[^97-3] ...
- c. the quantity and type of acquired thermal fuels;
- d. the applicable rate of the CO<sub>2</sub> levy.

<sup>3</sup> The FOCBS may demand further evidence if required for determining the refund. In particular, the invoices for the CO<sub>2</sub> levy paid must be produced if it so requests.[^97-4]

[^97-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^97-2]: The name of this administrative unit was changed on 1 Jan. 2022 pursuant to Art. 20 para. 2 of the Publications Ordinance of 7 Oct. 2015 ([SR 170.512.1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/cc/2015/670)) ([AS 2021 589](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2021/589)). This change has been made throughout the text.
[^97-3]: Repealed by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, with effect from 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^97-4]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).

#### Art. 98

**Periodicity of the refund[^98-1]**

<sup>1</sup> An application for refund shall cover a period of 12 months. It may cover a shorter period, provided that the amount requested is at least CHF 100,000.[^98-2]

<sup>2</sup>

It must be submitted by 30 June for the CO<sub>2</sub> levy paid for:

- a. the previous year;
- b. the fiscal year that ended in the previous year.

<sup>3</sup> A claim for refund is forfeited if the application is not submitted by the deadline.

[^98-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^98-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 24 Nov. 2021, in force since 1 Jan. 2022 ([AS 2021 859](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2021/859)).

#### Art. 98a

**Refund for CHP plant operators that are neither participating in the ETS nor are subject to a reduction obligation**[^98a-1]

<sup>1</sup> On request, an installation operator that is neither a participant in the ETS nor subject to a reduction obligation and which operates CHP plants in accordance with Article 32*a* paragraph 1 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act shall be refunded 60 per cent of the CO<sub>2</sub> levy on the fuels used for electricity production for each CHP plant which has a rated thermal input of at least 0.5 MW and no more than 20 MW.

<sup>2</sup>

The CHP plant operator is entitled to a refund of the remaining 40 per cent of the CO<sub>2</sub> levy on the fuels used to produce electricity if:

- a. it allocates this amount to the measures set out in Article 32b paragraph 2 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act;
- b. the measure effectively serves to increase energy efficiency;
- c. it does not implement the measures in a plant whose operator is subject to a reduction obligation or is participating in the ETS;
- d. it does not claim the effect of the measures elsewhere; and
- e. it implements the measures within three subsequent years.

<sup>3</sup> The FOEN may, on request, extend the period referred to in paragraph 2 letter e by two years.

[^98a-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 1 Nov. 2017 ([AS 2017 6753](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2017/757)). Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).

#### Art. 98b

**Application for a refund for CHP plant operators that are neither participating in the ETS nor subject to a reduction obligation[^98b-2]**[^98b-1]

<sup>1</sup>

CHP plant operators that are neither participating in the ETS nor subject to a reduction obligation shall submit the application for a refund for the attention of the enforcement authority by 30 June. In particular, it must contain:[^98b-3]

- a. the quantity of taxed fuels used for electricity production, calculated on the basis of the annual quantity of electricity shown on the guarantee of origin and of the calorific value of the energy source used;
- b. the proof of origin in accordance with Article 9 paragraph 1 EnA[^98b-4];
- c. information on the rated thermal input;
- d. the monitoring report;
- e. information on the annual development of the CO<sub>2</sub> emissions resulting from the measured production of electricity;
- f. confirmation from the local canton that the Ordinance on Air Pollution Control has been complied with;
- g. information on planned measures;
- [tab] h. and i.[^98b-5] ...
- j.[^98b-6] confirmation that thermal fuels on which the levy was paid were used to operate the CHP plants, together with details of the CO<sub>2</sub> levy rate applied.

<sup>2</sup> The FOEN shall specify the form of the application.

<sup>3</sup> It shall check compliance with the requirements specified in paragraph 1 letters a–g and forward the application to the FOCBS for a decision.

<sup>3bis</sup> Invoices for the CO<sub>2</sub> levy paid must be produced if the FOCBS so requests.[^98b-7]

<sup>4</sup> The monitoring report referred to in paragraph 1 letter d shall in particular contain information on the development of CO<sub>2</sub> emissions resulting from electricity production and a description of the measures and investments implemented. The FOEN specifies the form of the report.

[^98b-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 1 Nov. 2017, in force since 1 Jan. 2018 ([AS 2017 6753](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2017/757)).
[^98b-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^98b-3]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^98b-4]: [SR 730.0](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/cc/2017/762)
[^98b-5]: Repealed by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, with effect from 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^98b-6]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^98b-7]: Inserted by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, with effect from 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).

#### Art. 98c

**Periodicity of refunds for CHP plant operators that are neither participating in the ETS nor subject to a reduction obligation[^98c-2]**[^98c-1]

<sup>1</sup> Applications for a refund in accordance with Article 98*b* shall be submitted for a period of 12 months and apply to fuels consumed in the previous year or in the financial year that ended in the previous year.

<sup>2</sup> The refund is made by the FOCBS and covers 100 per cent of the CO<sub>2</sub> levy on the fuels used to produce electricity.

<sup>3</sup> The right to a refund is forfeited if the application is not submitted in due time.

[^98c-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 1 Nov. 2017, in force since 1 Jan. 2018 ([AS 2017 6753](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2017/757)).
[^98c-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).

#### Art. 99

**Refund for fuels not used to produce energy**

<sup>1</sup> Any person who has paid a levy on thermal fuels that were not used to produce energy and wants to apply for a refund must prove the quantity that was not used to produce energy. To this end, records (consumption control) must be maintained for the input, output and consumption of the thermal fuels as well as for warehouse stocks.

<sup>1bis</sup> The FOCBS may the refund the levy for thermal fuels not used to produce energy based on the purchased quantity, provided the applicant’s operating conditions leave no doubt that the use was not related to energy production and the applicant provides the FOCBS with binding confirmation that using the thermal fuels was not related to energy production.[^99-1]

<sup>2</sup> The refund application must be submitted to the FOCBS in the prescribed form.

<sup>3</sup>

It must contain information about:

- a. the form of use not related to energy production;
- b. the quantity and type of thermal fuels that were not used to produce energy;
- c. the applicable rate of the CO<sub>2</sub> levy.
